160macOS root service configuration and rotating logs followed inherited HOME before the principal gate — both the dedicated LaunchDaemon helper and the shared exact --service path loaded custom identity and initialized logging before start_os_service required effective UID 0. The macOS config and log helpers then derived credential/configuration and file-log directories through ambient HOME. The installed LaunchDaemon normally supplies a controlled environment, but a UID-0 manual, recovery, diagnostic, or future launcher invocation could redirect privileged durable state into a caller-selected and potentially ordinary-user-writable home. This was concrete privileged configuration/credential/log namespace confused-deputy authority under an already UID-0 invocation, not evidence that an ordinary user could alter the installed LaunchDaemon or gain root.FIX R-S11al/R-S11e-52 centralizes both macOS service entries. It proves numeric effective UID 0 first, resolves that UID's protected home through getpwuid_r, then loads the signed custom identity, initializes one immutable config/log root matching the existing macOS directory mapping, initializes logging, and finally enters the independently UID-gated service listener. Config::get_home, Config::path, and Config::log_path use the stored service root while every ordinary macOS user process retains ambient user-owned path behavior. Pure path regression, source, Apple-conformance, and semantic mutation gates bind the complete ordering and both entries; native macOS and signed-artifact evidence remains with R-R2/R-B2.
161Unexpected desktop IPC listener loss was reported as a successful process shutdown — all six process-lifetime desktop listener streams distinguished unexpected end-of-stream from requested cancellation and initiated the shared bounded drain, but the finalizer always called exit(0). Losing main/password IPC, Unix protected service/password IPC, or Windows service control/credential IPC therefore produced a false-success terminal status; for the shipped systemd Restart=on-failure topology this could suppress automatic recovery. This was deterministic lifecycle/recovery and monitoring correctness, not a demonstrated remote listener-termination primitive or privilege escalation.FIX R-S11am/R-S11e-53 adds one monotonic release/acquire failure latch. Each of the six unexpected listener-end branches sets it before cancellation and the existing transaction/mutation drain. R-S11as now makes the sole retained desktop owner join desktop IPC and invoke the one finalizer, which exits 1 when failure was latched and 0 only for ordinary shutdown; protected Unix service IPC returns its post-drain error to its separate foreground owner under R-S11ar. Later clean requests cannot downgrade failure. Source/Apple gates and semantic mutations bind all six producers and both owner forms. Native Windows/macOS injection and exact installed-artifact evidence remain R-R2/R-B2.
162The Linux root service detached protected IPC startup and shutdown from supervisor ownership — _service and raw _service_password ran inside thread::spawn(|| allow_err!(ipc::start(...))). Parent-directory, listener-bind, socket-mode, or listener-guard failure was discarded and the root supervisor could still launch its controlled child without the protected administration boundary. SIGTERM/SIGINT and internal supervisor-loop errors returned without cancelling or joining the IPC thread, allowing process exit to abort an admitted polkit/password transaction even though the ledger claimed a complete non-aborting Linux drain. This was deterministic fail-open service availability/recovery and privileged-operation finality, not evidence that an unprivileged peer bypassed the receiver's separate credential/action authorization.FIX R-S11an/R-S11e-54 makes the root supervisor retain a fallibly created named IPC thread. A bounded channel reports success only after both protected sockets and their process-lifetime guard are active; any setup error, timeout, disconnection, unexpected return, error, or panic fails the supervisor before child selection. The signal path closes IPC admission, the live loop observes premature worker termination, and every loop result converges on cancellation plus exact worker join before either service-owned child is terminated. The join has no artificial transaction-drain timeout, so admitted password administration retains its child until the receiver-owned ledger is drained. Focused channel/outcome tests, an exact source gate, and independent mutations bind the closure; native installed-service injection and exact artifact evidence remain with R-R2/R-B2.
163The macOS root LaunchDaemon did not translate launchd termination into protected-IPC cancellation — both root-service entrypoints converged on the synchronous _service/_service_password future, whose cancellation branch correctly owned complete generic/password transaction and replay-ledger drain, but neither entry installed a termination handler. The only Unix Tokio signal task ran in the separate controlled-side server process. A launchd SIGTERM therefore kept its default process-termination disposition and could destroy an admitted Authorization Services/password mutation before that receiver-owned drain ran. This was deterministic privileged-operation finality and service-stop correctness, not a peer-authorization bypass or proof of remote signal authority.FIX R-S11ao/R-S11e-55 installs the termination-enabled handler after numeric root proof and before either protected listener. Registration failure prevents listener admission; the callback performs only the process cancellation request and does not set the fatal-listener-loss latch. The existing listener owner then closes admission, joins accepted work, drains and clears the password ledger in order, drops its guard, and returns normally. Shared and Apple source gates plus independent mutations bind both entrypoints, ordering, callback narrowness, and drain preservation. Native launchd stop/upgrade and exact signed-artifact evidence remain with R-R2/R-B2.
164The desktop controlled-side server detached both termination receipt and its sole public listener from lifecycle ownership — Linux service children, the macOS LaunchAgent server, and Windows service-owned servers spawned direct_server without retaining its Tokio JoinHandle, spawned a second unowned signal task only after the listener could bind, and parked the parent future forever. Listener clean return or unwind could therefore leave main IPC and the process falsely healthy with no public service. Unix signal-registration failure was logged and forgotten. This was deterministic service availability/recovery and admitted-session stop correctness, not a PAKE bypass, remote task-termination primitive, or evidence of compromise.FIX R-S11ap/R-S11e-56 makes desktop start_server(true) construct fallible platform signal streams before IPC/public admission and transfer them to a retained lifecycle owner. R-S11as/R-S11e-59 extends that owner to desktop IPC readiness/completion. It retains the exact public task, selects cancellation/signals/public/IPC completion, joins the exact task and native IPC thread, and only then invokes the sole finalizer. Signal-stream or unexpected worker/listener completion latches status 1. Detached signal/IPC tasks and desktop parking are absent; Android's foreground-service generation boundary is unchanged. Native supervisor stop and exact-artifact evidence remain R-R2/R-B2.
165A losing desktop graceful-shutdown caller could terminate the process while the winning detached IPC finalizer was still draining — R-S11ap added a main-thread finalizer call after exact public-listener join, but the older atomic idempotence guard returned immediately to any caller that lost ownership. On Linux and Windows that return could unwind the main server runtime while an IPC thread still drained. This was shutdown finality/service recovery correctness, not an authentication bypass, local privilege escalation, or evidence of compromise.FIX R-S11aq/R-S11e-57 first made crate-private finish_graceful_shutdown return ! and made losers wait. R-S11as/R-S11e-59 removes the competing callers entirely: main and Windows service-main IPC return to the retained desktop owner, which joins public and IPC work before the finalizer's sole call. The obsolete election, follower wait, returning compatibility entry, and polling IPC barrier are absent. Shared/Apple gates and semantic mutations bind sole-call and join-before-finalizer order. Native supervisor and exact-artifact evidence remain R-R2/R-B2.
166Protected Unix service IPC bypassed its retained lifecycle owner after listener failure — R-S11an retained the Linux protected IPC worker so its supervisor could observe error/panic/return, join its complete drain, and then terminate exact controlled children. The shared Linux/macOS receiver nevertheless called the process-exit finalizer after listener loss, preventing that Linux join and bypassing macOS's synchronous result. This was deterministic cleanup, service recovery, and lifecycle-authority composition failure, not an IPC authorization bypass or privilege escalation.FIX R-S11ar/R-S11e-58 makes the protected receiver return its recorded listener error only after transaction/password-state drain and guard drop; ordinary signal cancellation returns success after the same drain. Linux receives the error through its retained join and still attempts both exact child terminations; macOS propagates it through both service entrypoints. Protected service IPC remains excluded from R-S11as's sole desktop finalizer caller. Focused outcome, shared/Apple, and semantic mutation gates bind the transfer. Native supervisor/launchd injection and exact-artifact evidence remain R-R2/R-B2.
167Desktop authority-bearing IPC started before mandatory invariants and remained detached from public-service lifecycle ownership — ordinary main IPC and the Windows service-owned credential/control pair ran on independent infallibly spawned native threads. They began before R-A4 startup checks, reported no readiness, could independently finalize the process, and could not be observed or joined by the retained public-listener owner. A service could therefore admit local authority before mandatory policy proof, start its public path without all required local endpoints, or terminate while another runtime still owned admitted work. This was deterministic local-authority admission, service-health, and cleanup correctness; the listeners retained their separate peer-authorization gates, so it is not evidence of an authorization bypass, privilege escalation, or compromise.FIX R-S11as/R-S11e-59 moves desktop invariants before admission and creates one fallible named native IPC worker. A single current-thread Tokio runtime prepares all required main/password and Windows service-main listeners, reports readiness once every guard is active, then returns its complete post-drain outcome. The existing signal owner remains active during readiness and starts the public listener only afterward. One async owner selects cancellation, signals, public-listener completion, and IPC completion, then joins the exact public task and native thread through spawn_blocking before the sole finalizer. Detached IPC spawns, the independent Windows runtime, unused server-running flag, finalizer election/follower wait, and polling IPC barrier are deleted. Shared/Apple gates, pinned Linux compilation, and a semantic mutation matrix bind the closure; native init/launchd/SCM injection and exact-artifact evidence remain R-R2/R-B2.
168World-connectable Linux protected sockets performed root active-session work before bounded admission — both _service and raw _service_password invoked the fresh seat lookup before acquiring their fixed transaction semaphore. Any local principal able to connect could therefore induce synchronous root loginctl/login1 work outside the admission budget, even though the later kernel-UID or executable check rejected it. This was a concrete local resource-exhaustion and service-availability edge, not an authorization bypass, privilege escalation, remote trigger, or evidence of compromise.FIX R-S11at/R-S11e-60 acquires the endpoint's existing transaction permit before all active-session and executable-identity work and carries it through dispatch. Root skips the irrelevant active-session lookup. Non-root peers reach the fresh final-authority lookup only after matching a cached-only active UID; missing or nonmatching cache state rejects without live work and never grants authority. A just-switched-out peer still fails the fresh check, while a just-switched-in peer fails closed until the service cache refreshes. The stale alternate lookup helper is removed. Focused Rust policy coverage, a standalone semantic validator, source gates, and independent mutations bind both endpoint orders, the negative-only cache model, fresh final authority, and exact permit lifetime; native installed-service contention and exact-artifact evidence remain R-R2/R-B2.
169macOS accepted a stale or mix-and-match same-Team privileged helper — daemon status treated the two installed plists as sufficient even when an app upgrade left the prior helper under /Library/PrivilegedHelperTools. Install and runtime checks proved the helper's Team ID/identifier and install-time equality with the caller-supplied bundled path, but did not bind that path or the deployed bytes to the currently installed app's recursively validated outer signature. This was a privileged-component version/provenance coherence defect and potential downgrade to defects present in an older signed helper; it is not evidence that a downgrade, privilege escalation, or compromise occurred.FIX R-S11au/R-S11e-61 makes the installed signed app the build authority. Static app validation is strict, nested, and all-architecture; helper validation is all-architecture. The admin installer accepts only the fixed installed Contents/MacOS/service, validates the root-owned non-writable outer app before copy, and immediately before launch revalidates the app plus deployed/bundled byte equality. Runtime helper trust requires that same app proof and performs a no-follow descriptor-bound, fixed-buffer streaming equality check. Plist-only stale state now returns not-current and exposes reinstall, while uninstall accepts any managed partial/stale artifact. A focused regression, semantic validator, 26-mutation matrix, shared/Apple gates, and ledger bind the closure; native signed-bundle upgrade evidence and exact-artifact proof remain R-R2/R-B2.
170macOS creation calls passed narrow mode_t values directly to variadic openat — Darwin defines mode_t as a 16-bit integer, but C default argument promotion applies at the ellipsis. The secure Unix config transaction and both macOS clipboard creation paths therefore failed Rust's Apple-target type check with E0617 before native execution could be evaluated. This was a deterministic Apple build/ABI correctness defect and unreachable security-hardening code, not evidence of privilege escalation, runtime exposure, data corruption, or compromise.FIX R-S11av/R-S11e-62 promotes exactly the three variadic creation-mode arguments to c_uint, matching the existing portable file-transfer helper. The already-promoted i32 PID-file literal remains unchanged. Fixed-signature mkdir/mkdirat/fchmod calls retain mode_t; exclusive/no-follow flags, requested modes, descriptor checks, and cleanup are unchanged. A standalone semantic validator, 13-mutation matrix, shared/Apple gates, and the SDK-free Apple compiler cross-check bind the source closure. Native SDK-backed behavior and exact signed-artifact evidence remain R-R2/R-B2.

Appendix D · Dependency advisories — gated disposition ledger

The dependency-advisory posture is now implemented as a repeatable gate. The Rust side is scripts/audit.sh: a digest-pinned Rust 1.75 audit image installs the pinned cargo-audit and cargo-deny versions from scripts/pins.env, bakes the pinned RustSec ADVISORY_DB_COMMIT, parses only deny.toml [advisories].ignore objects as accepts, and fails on any unignored advisory. The Dart side is scripts/dart-audit.sh: pinned osv-scanner runs offline against flutter/pubspec.lock and the currently-empty reason-bearing accept-list. Native vcpkg C/C++ codec libraries are outside RustSec/Pub OSV and are tracked by docs/NATIVE-CODEC-WATCH.md plus scripts/native-codec-watch.sh.

AreaDispositionEnforcement
Rust lockfile fixesFixed in the committed graph where the pinned Rust 1.75 build allowed it: url 2.5.4 removes the idna 0.5.0 RUSTSEC-2024-0421 path; rand 0.8.6 removes the RUSTSEC-2026-0097 CSPRNG issue; crossbeam-channel 0.5.15 and tracing-subscriber 0.3.20 remove their reported advisories; the in-tree users dependency surface is the maintained uzers 0.12.2 package.Cargo.lock plus the Rust advisory gate. Comment-only RUSTSEC mentions in deny.toml are not ignore flags.
Rust reasoned acceptsThe remaining RustSec findings are accepted only in deny.toml, each as an { id, reason } object. The accepted set covers the Rust-1.75/MSRV-blocked OpenSSL advisories, platform-pruned or build/dev-only findings such as time and libgit2-sys, compile-time or radio-silence unmaintained crates, the load-bearing sodiumoxide crypto dependency, the retained Linux fuser CLIPRDR path with its service/mount bounds, the GTK/unic clusters, and other transitive unmaintained warnings whose reachable surface is documented in the reason.scripts/audit.sh turns only real TOML ignore entries into cargo-audit --ignore flags; cargo-deny check advisories reads the same deny.toml policy against the same pinned advisory snapshot.
Dart/PubThe resolved Flutter/Pub graph is OSV-clean against the pinned Pub snapshot. The accept-list exists but has no active entries.scripts/dart-audit.sh requires scripts/dart-audit-ignores.txt, rejects malformed accepts or accepts without reasons, and runs osv-scanner --offline --lockfile=flutter/pubspec.lock.
Native vcpkg C/C++ librariesRustSec and Pub OSV do not cover libvpx, libyuv, opus, libjpeg-turbo, oboe, or cpu-features. Open runtime-reachable decoder advisories remain part of Appendix C #2b's accepted in-process viewer residual until a future decoder sandbox or pin bump closes them. aom is retired by Appendix C #88, and AV1/libaom remains runtime-quarantined by Appendix C #87 as wire-level policy only.scripts/native-codec-watch.sh verifies the exact vcpkg package set, overlay pins, current requirements hash, retired-aom record, and native-codec watch ledger. It is a watch/sync gate, not a decoder sandbox.
Release gate shapeThe Windows harness, online-input mutation, Android Gradle execution-cache, Android Rust target check, Apple source-conformance, Rust-advisory, Dart-advisory, native-codec, runtime-smoke, Flutter-analysis, and build-harness fail-loud gates are intentionally slower than the day-to-day verifier, but release verification runs them. The fast verifier pins the release-gate shape so the slow gate cannot silently lose, duplicate, add, or reorder a checker, stop using pins, drop platform-specific source conformance, or return to grep-based accepts. Release-gate text scans use one fixed root-owned GNU grep and treat scanner errors distinctly from a clean no-match; the closed release preflight proves that scanner before copying the online closure.scripts/verify-release.sh includes exactly, in order, verify.sh, verify-windows-harness.py --self-test, online-input-provenance.py --self-test, test-android-gradle-cache.sh, android-rust-check.sh, smoke-server.sh, dart-verify.sh, native-codec-watch.sh, apple-conform-check.sh, audit.sh, dart-audit.sh, and test-build-faillo.sh; scripts/verify.sh asserts the Apple-gate structure and that exact ordered twelve-gate release bundle.

This ledger does not claim dependency perfection. It claims repeatability and explicit ownership: a dependency advisory is either fixed in the locked graph, accepted in a machine-readable policy with a reason, outside the shipped role, or tracked in the native-codec residual ledger that Rust/Dart package tools cannot cover.

requirements.html — normative security requirements for a hardened RustDesk fork. Base pinned at upstream 1.4.7 (0c86d46) + hbb_common@df6badc; build pinned by the committed Cargo.lock (909 package records; 37 git-sourced records from 27 unique source URLs, all rev-locked), the provenance-recorded in-tree portable-pty, and the toolchain of §3.1. Every architectural and surface claim was verified by direct read of that tree. The bar is SSH. Corrections, adversarial review of the §10 handshake, and an independent audit of the chosen PAKE implementation are actively welcomed.
